### 19. user (2026-07-09T03:45:39.940Z)

1	# VS Code Agent Session Router — Architecture & Implementation Plan
2	
3	> **🏁 CHECKPOINT 1 — 2026-07-08** ✅ Merged  
4	> Phase 1 (Foundation) complete.  
5	>
6	> **🏁 CHECKPOINT 2 — 2026-07-08** ✅ Merged  
7	> Phase 2 (Copilot extractor) + Phase 3 (Watcher).  
8	>
9	> **🏁 CHECKPOINT 3 — 2026-07-08** ✅ Merged  
10	> Phase 5 (Continue + Cline). Pluggable architecture. Cross-repo ingestion.  
11	> 7 agent discoverers + extractors. 84 tests passing.  
12	> Extension packaged as `.vsix`, installed and operational.  
13	>
14	> **Next (optional)**: Phase 6 suite expansion (handoff generator, search, diff, CI).  
15	> **Repo**: <https://github.com/avidullu/agent-session-router>
16	
17	## 1. Problem Statement
18	
19	The **Agent Sessions** repo (`Projects/Agent Sessions`) is a Python-based private
20	archive for coding-agent sessions. It discovers, extracts, and exports agent
21	transcripts as Markdown/PDF files. Currently it supports Codex, Claude, Gemini,
22	Grok, and DeepSeek — but **VS Code Copilot Chat and other VS Code-based agents
23	have no live routing mechanism**. Their session data is scattered across
24	`globalStorage` and `workspaceStorage` directories that the Python tool can only
25	treat as static inventory.
26	
27	### 1.1 What the Agent Sessions repo does today
28	
29	| Step | Mechanism |
30	|------|-----------|
31	| **Discovery** | TOML-configured source roots with glob patterns |
32	| **Extraction** | Per-agent extractors (`@register("kind")`) that parse raw files → `ExtractedSession` |
33	| **Rendering** | `render.py` → Markdown with metadata header + transcript |
34	| **Indexing** | `archive/index.jsonl` + `archive/INDEX.md` |
35	| **Baseline** | Knowledge/replay system for analyzing archived sessions |
36	
37	### 1.2 The gap
38	
39	- **Copilot Chat**: listed as `inventory`-only. Sessions are in
40	  `workspaceStorage/*/GitHub.copilot-chat/debug-logs/` (JSONL) and
41	  `chat-session-resources/` but no extractor exists.
42	- **DeepSeek V4**: has a `deepseek_request_dump` extractor, but only captures raw
43	  request text — no structured conversation, no metadata enrichment.
44	- **No live routing**: the Python tool is batch-oriented. A VS Code extension
45	  can provide real-time or near-real-time routing of finished sessions.
46	
47	---
48	
49	## 2. Vision: The VS Code Agent Session Router Extension
50	
51	A VS Code extension that acts as a **bridge** between VS Code's internal agent
52	session storage and the Agent Sessions archive pipeline. It is the first in a
53	suite of helper tools for processing agent session handoffs and transcripts.
54	
55	### 2.1 Core capabilities
56	
57	1. **Discover** — scan VS Code's `globalStorage` and `workspaceStorage` for
58	   agent session files (Copilot Chat, DeepSeek, and any future agent extensions).
59	2. **Extract** — parse raw session formats into structured conversation
60	   transcripts (user ↔ agent turns with tool calls).
61	3. **Render** — produce Markdown files in the exact format the Agent Sessions
62	   repo expects (metadata header + numbered transcript sections).
63	4. **Route** — place rendered Markdown files into a configurable output
64	   directory (default: the Agent Sessions `archive/` dir or a staging folder).
65	5. **Watch** — optional filesystem watcher that auto-exports sessions as they
66	   are completed or modified.
67	
68	### 2.2 Extension architecture
69	
70	```
71	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
72	│                 VS Code Extension Host                    │
73	│                                                          │
74	│  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐              │
75	│  │ Discover │  │ Extract  │  │  Render  │              │
76	│  │  Module  │  │  Module  │  │  Module  │              │
77	│  └────┬─────┘  └────┬─────┘  └────┬─────┘              │
78	│       │              │              │                    │
79	│  ┌────┴──────────────┴──────────────┴────┐              │
80	│  │           Router / Orchestrator        │              │
81	│  └────┬──────────────────────────────┬────┘              │
82	│       │                              │                    │
83	│  ┌────┴─────┐                  ┌─────┴─────┐            │
84	│  │  Watcher  │                  │  Commands  │            │
85	│  │ (optional)│                  │  & Config  │            │
86	│  └──────────┘                  └───────────┘            │
87	│                                                          │
88	└──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘
89	                       │
90	                       ▼
91	         ┌─────────────────────────┐
92	         │   Agent Sessions Repo    │
93	         │   archive/**/*.md        │
94	         │   archive/index.jsonl    │
95	         └─────────────────────────┘
96	```
97	
98	### 2.3 Agent taxonomy — four categories
99	
100	The extension must handle a diverse ecosystem of AI coding agents. They fall into
101	four categories based on WHERE and HOW they store session data:
102	
103	#### Category A: Native VS Code Extensions (persist to VS Code storage)
104	
105	These are first-class VS Code extensions that use VS Code's storage APIs.
106	Session data lives in `globalStorage` or `workspaceStorage`.
107	
108	| Agent | Extension ID | Storage Location | Format | Status |
109	|-------|-------------|-----------------|--------|--------|
110	| **Copilot Chat** | `github.copilot-chat` | `workspaceStorage/*/github.copilot-chat/debug-logs/` | JSONL | ✅ Discoverer exists |
111	| **Copilot Chat** | `github.copilot-chat` | `workspaceStorage/*/github.copilot-chat/chat-session-resources/` | Per-turn `.txt` | 🔧 Phase 2 |
112	| **DeepSeek V4** | `vizards.deepseek-v4-for-copilot` | `globalStorage/vizards.deepseek-v4-for-copilot/request-dumps/` | JSON | ✅ Full support |
113	| **Z.AI / ZAI** | `ltmoerdani.zai-copilot-chat` | Reuses Copilot Chat storage (model-provider fork) | JSONL | ✅ Via copilot_chat |
114	| **OpenAI ChatGPT** | `openai.chatgpt` | Uses bundled Codex CLI → `~/.codex/sessions/` | JSONL | ✅ Via Codex extractor |
115	| **Grok (X.AI)** | *(VS Code LM API)* | Uses Copilot Chat storage via VS Code's "Set X.AI API Key" mechanism. All transcripts show `producer: copilot-agent` — model choice is not recorded in metadata. | JSONL | ✅ Via copilot_chat |
116	| **Any LM API key model** | *(VS Code LM API)* | Same as above — Claude, Gemini, Grok, or any model added via VS Code's "Set API Key" command routes through Copilot Chat's transcript storage. No separate discoverer needed. | JSONL | ✅ Via copilot_chat |
117	| **Gemini (VS Code)** | (TBD) | (TBD) | (TBD) | 🔮 Research needed |
118	
119	#### Category B: Aggregator-Backed VS Code Extensions
120	
121	These are VS Code extensions that connect to multiple LLM backends (Ollama,
122	OpenRouter, LM Studio, etc.). The backend is irrelevant to session storage —
123	the extension itself persists sessions.
124	
125	| Agent | Extension ID | Backends | Storage Location | Format | Status |
126	|-------|-------------|----------|-----------------|--------|--------|
127	| **Continue** | `continue.continue` | Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic, OpenRouter, LM Studio, etc. | `~/.continue/sessions/` | JSON | 🔧 Planned |
128	| **Cline** | `saoudrizwan.claude-dev` | Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic, OpenRouter, etc. | `globalStorage/saoudrizwan.claude-dev/` | JSON/JSONL | 🔧 Planned |
129	| **Cody** | `sourcegraph.cody` | Sourcegraph + BYOK | `globalStorage/sourcegraph.cody/` | Custom | 🔧 Planned |
130	| **Roo Cline** | `rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline` | Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic, etc. | `globalStorage/rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline/` | JSON/JSONL | 🔧 Planned |
131	| **Aider (via VS Code)** | `aider.aider-vscode` | Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic, etc. | Project `.aider*` files | Markdown/JSON | 🔧 Planned |
132	| **Tabby** | `tabbyml.tabby` | Self-hosted | `globalStorage/tabbyml.tabby/` | Custom | 🔧 Planned |
133	| **Codeium** | `codeium.codeium` | Proprietary | `globalStorage/codeium.codeium/` | Custom | 🔧 Planned |
134	| **Augment** | `augmentcode.augment` | Proprietary | `globalStorage/augmentcode.augment/` | Custom | 🔧 Planned |
135	| **Supermaven** | `supermaven.supermaven` | Proprietary | `globalStorage/supermaven.supermaven/` | Custom | 🔧 Planned |
136	| **Amazon Q** | `amazonwebservices.amazon-q-vscode` | AWS Bedrock | `globalStorage/amazonwebservices.amazon-q-vscode/` | Custom | 🔧 Planned |
137	
138	#### Category C: Model Servers & API Aggregators (NOT VS Code extensions)
139	
140	These do NOT store chat sessions themselves. They are backends that Category B
141	extensions connect to. They are listed here for completeness — the
142	agent-session-router does NOT interact with them directly.
143	
144	| Server | Type | Notes |
145	|--------|------|-------|
146	| **Ollama** | Local model server | Runs Llama, Mistral, Qwen, DeepSeek, etc. locally. No session storage. |
147	| **LM Studio** | Local model server | GUI + API for local models. No session storage. |
148	| **vLLM** | Local model server | High-throughput serving. No session storage. |
149	| **LocalAI** | Local model server | OpenAI-compatible local API. No session storage. |
150	| **OpenRouter** | API aggregator | Unified API for 200+ models. No session storage. |
151	| **Groq** | API aggregator | Fast inference API. No session storage. |
152	| **Together AI** | API aggregator | Open-source model API. No session storage. |
153	| **Fireworks AI** | API aggregator | Fast model serving API. No session storage. |
154	| **DeepInfra** | API aggregator | Hosted open-source models. No session storage. |
155	| **AWS Bedrock** | API aggregator | Managed foundation models. No session storage. |
156	| **Google Vertex AI** | API aggregator | Managed ML platform. No session storage. |
157	
158	#### Category D: Terminal/CLI Tools (persist outside VS Code)
159	
160	These are standalone CLI tools used alongside VS Code. Sessions are stored
161	independently of VS Code. Already covered by the Agent Sessions Python tool.
162	
163	| Tool | Storage Location | Format | Agent Sessions Status |
164	|------|-----------------|--------|----------------------|
165	| **Claude Code** | `~/.claude/projects/{project}/*.jsonl` | JSONL | ✅ Supported |
166	| **Codex CLI** | `~/.codex/sessions/` | JSONL | ✅ Supported |
167	| **Gemini CLI** | `~/.gemini/antigravity/brain/` | JSONL | ✅ Supported |
168	| **Grok CLI** | `~/.grok/sessions/` | JSONL | ✅ Supported |
169	| **Aider CLI** | Project `.aider*` files | Markdown/JSON | 🔧 Planned (Agent Sessions) |
170	| **Qwen CLI** | (TBD) | (TBD) | 🔮 Research needed |
171	| **Kimi CLI** | (TBD) | (TBD) | 🔮 Research needed |
172	
173	### 2.4 Why aggregators are transparent to the architecture
174	
175	The key insight: **model backends (Ollama, OpenRouter, LM Studio) are just API
176	endpoints**. They do not persist chat sessions. The VS Code extension that uses
177	them (Continue, Cline, etc.) is responsible for session storage.
178	
179	This means the agent-session-router needs discoverers/extractors for the VS Code
180	EXTENSIONS (Category B), not for the backends (Category C). Once an extension's
181	storage format is understood, the router handles it identically regardless of
182	which backend model was used.
183	
184	```
185	┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────────┐     ┌─────────────────┐
186	│  Ollama       │     │  Continue.dev     │     │  agent-session- │
187	│  (backend)    │◄────│  (VS Code ext)    │────►│  router          │
188	│  No storage   │     │  ~/.continue/     │     │  → archive/*.md │
189	└──────────────┘     └──────────────────┘     └─────────────────┘
190	
191	┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────────┐     ┌─────────────────┐
192	│  OpenRouter   │     │  Cline            │     │  agent-session- │
193	│  (backend)    │◄────│  (VS Code ext)    │────►│  router          │
194	│  No storage   │     │  globalStorage/   │     │  → archive/*.md │
195	└──────────────┘     └──────────────────┘     └─────────────────┘
196	```
197	
198	Adding a new aggregator-backed extension always follows the same 3-step recipe:
199	1. **Install** the extension + run a test session
200	2. **Locate** its session storage (check `globalStorage/{id}/`, `~/.{name}/`)
201	3. **Write** a discoverer + extractor (~50 lines of TypeScript each)
202	
203	---
204	
205	## 3. Detailed Implementation Plan
206	
207	### Phase 1: Foundation — Extension Scaffold & Core Pipeline
208	
209	**Goal**: Working extension that can discover, extract, and render sessions from
210	at least one source (DeepSeek), producing Markdown compatible with the Agent
211	Sessions repo.
212	
213	#### 3.1 Project structure
214	
215	```
216	extensions/
217	├── agent-session-router/          # VS Code extension
218	│   ├── .vscode/
219	│   │   ├── launch.json
220	│   │   └── tasks.json
221	│   ├── src/
222	│   │   ├── extension.ts           # Entry point: activate/deactivate
223	│   │   ├── config.ts              # Extension configuration
224	│   │   ├── discoverers/
225	│   │   │   ├── index.ts           # Discovery registry
226	│   │   │   ├── copilot-chat.ts    # Copilot Chat session discovery
227	│   │   │   ├── deepseek.ts        # DeepSeek request-dump discovery
228	│   │   │   ├── continue.ts        # Continue.dev sessions (Phase 5)
229	│   │   │   ├── cline.ts           # Cline task history (Phase 5)
230	│   │   │   └── cody.ts            # Cody chat history (Phase 5)
231	│   │   ├── extractors/
232	│   │   │   ├── index.ts           # Extractor registry
233	│   │   │   ├── copilot-chat.ts    # Copilot JSONL → messages
234	│   │   │   ├── deepseek.ts        # DeepSeek JSON → messages
235	│   │   │   ├── continue.ts        # Continue JSON → messages (Phase 5)
236	│   │   │   ├── cline.ts           # Cline JSONL → messages (Phase 5)
237	│   │   │   └── cody.ts            # Cody format → messages (Phase 5)
238	│   │   ├── renderers/
239	│   │   │   ├── index.ts           # Renderer registry
240	│   │   │   └── markdown.ts        # Markdown renderer (matching Agent Sessions format)
241	│   │   ├── router.ts              # Orchestrator: discover → extract → render → write
242	│   │   ├── watcher.ts             # Filesystem watcher (chokidar)
243	│   │   ├── commands.ts            # VS Code command registrations
244	│   │   └── utils.ts               # Shared utilities
245	│   ├── test/
246	│   │   ├── extension.test.ts
247	│   │   ├── discoverers.test.ts
248	│   │   ├── extractors.test.ts
249	│   │   └── renderers.test.ts
250	│   ├── package.json
251	│   ├── tsconfig.json
252	│   ├── .vscodeignore
253	│   └── README.md
254	├── PLAN.md                        # This document
255	└── .gitignore
256	```
257	
258	#### 3.2 Key design decisions
259	
260	1. **Extractor registry pattern** — same as Agent Sessions' `@register("kind")`
261	   decorator, but in TypeScript. Each agent source gets a registered extractor.
262	2. **Markdown format compatibility** — the renderer MUST produce the exact
263	   format that Agent Sessions expects:
264	   ```markdown
265	   # {source_name} / {session_id}
266	   ## Metadata
267	   - Source: `{source_name}`
268	   - Kind: `{source_kind}`
269	   - Source file: `{path}`
270	   - SHA-256: `{digest}`
271	   - Source modified: `{timestamp}`
272	   - Imported at: `{timestamp}`
273	   ## Transcript
274	   ### 1. user (2024-01-01T00:00:00Z)
275	   {message text}
276	   ### 2. assistant (2024-01-01T00:00:01Z)
277	   {message text}
278	   ```
279	3. **Output routing** — configurable destination:
280	   - `agentSessions.archiveDir`: direct write into Agent Sessions archive
281	   - `agentSessions.stagingDir`: write to a staging area for manual review
282	   - `agentSessions.outputDir`: generic output directory
283	4. **Idempotent exports** — use SHA-256 of source file + size+mtime to skip
284	   already-exported sessions (same as Agent Sessions' `_can_reuse_record`).
285	5. **Extension runs purely in VS Code** — no Python dependency. The Agent
286	   Sessions repo does its own post-processing (indexing, PDF, baseline).
287	
288	#### 3.3 Commands
289	
290	| Command | Description |
291	|---------|-------------|
292	| `agentSessionRouter.discover` | Scan and report all discoverable agent sessions |
293	| `agentSessionRouter.exportAll` | Export all discovered sessions to Markdown |
294	| `agentSessionRouter.exportSession` | Export a specific session file |
295	| `agentSessionRouter.watchStart` | Start filesystem watcher for auto-export |
296	| `agentSessionRouter.watchStop` | Stop filesystem watcher |
297	| `agentSessionRouter.showConfig` | Show current extension configuration |
298	
299	#### 3.4 Configuration (contributes.configuration)
300	
301	```jsonc
302	{
303	  "agentSessionRouter.enabled": true,
304	  "agentSessionRouter.outputDir": "~/Projects/Agent Sessions/archive",
305	  "agentSessionRouter.sources": {
306	    // Category A: Native VS Code Extensions
307	    "copilotChat": { "enabled": true },
308	    "deepseek": { "enabled": true },
309	    "openaiChatGPT": { "enabled": true },
310	    "gemini": { "enabled": false },
311	    // Category A forks (share storage with parent)
312	    "zai": { "enabled": true, "note": "Z.AI reuses Copilot Chat storage" },
313	    // Category B: Aggregator-Backed Extensions
314	    "continue": { "enabled": false },
315	    "cline": { "enabled": false },
316	    "cody": { "enabled": false },
317	    "rooCline": { "enabled": false },
318	    "aiderVscode": { "enabled": false },
319	    "tabby": { "enabled": false },
320	    "codeium": { "enabled": false },
321	    "augment": { "enabled": false },
322	    "supermaven": { "enabled": false },
323	    "amazonQ": { "enabled": false }
324	  },
325	  "agentSessionRouter.watch": {
326	    "enabled": false,
327	    "debounceMs": 5000
328	  },
329	  "agentSessionRouter.maxSessionAge": "90d"
330	}
331	```
332	
333	### Phase 2: Copilot Chat Extractor
334	
335	**Goal**: Build a proper extractor for VS Code Copilot Chat sessions.
336	
337	**Challenge**: Copilot Chat session data is split across:
338	- `debug-logs/{uuid}/main.jsonl` — session timeline events
339	- `debug-logs/{uuid}/models.json` — model/tool metadata
340	- `chat-session-resources/{uuid}/call_*__vscode-*/content.txt` — actual message content
341	
342	The extractor needs to:
343	1. Discover session UUIDs from `debug-logs/` directories
344	2. Parse `main.jsonl` for the session timeline (user messages, assistant responses, tool calls)
345	3. Cross-reference `chat-session-resources/` for actual message content
346	4. Reconstruct full conversation turns
347	
348	### Phase 3: Watcher & Auto-Export
349	
350	**Goal**: Optional real-time routing using `chokidar` or VS Code's
351	`FileSystemWatcher`.
352	
353	- Watch `workspaceStorage` and `globalStorage` for new/modified session files
354	- Debounce (default 5s) to avoid partial writes
355	- Auto-export completed sessions to the configured output directory
356	
357	### Phase 4: DeepSeek Enhanced Extractor
358	
359	**Goal**: Upgrade the DeepSeek extractor from raw request dump to structured
360	conversation.
361	
362	DeepSeek's `request-dumps/` stores:
363	- `deepseek-provider-input-*.json` — full provider input with system prompt + messages
364	- `deepseek-request-*.input.json` — the actual request body
365	- `deepseek-request-*.json` — the request metadata
366	
367	Enhanced extractor should:
368	1. Parse the provider input to extract the full message array
369	2. Separate system prompt, user messages, and assistant responses
370	3. Include tool call/result pairs as structured sections
371	
372	### Phase 5: Aggregator-Backed Agent Support
373	
374	**Goal**: Add discoverers and extractors for all major aggregator-backed VS Code
375	extensions (Category B). Each follows the same recipe: install → locate storage →
376	write discoverer + extractor.
377	
378	**Priority order** (by popularity + likelihood of disk persistence):
379	
380	| Priority | Agent | Why First |
381	|----------|-------|-----------|
382	| P1 | **Continue.dev** | Most popular open-source AI extension; known to persist to `~/.continue/sessions/` as JSON |
383	| P1 | **Cline** | Fast-growing; persists task history to globalStorage |
384	| P2 | **Cody** | Sourcegraph's assistant; enterprise adoption |
385	| P2 | **Aider (VS Code)** | Terminal tool with VS Code integration; `.aider*` files in project dir |
386	| P3 | **Tabby** | Self-hosted; growing corporate adoption |
387	| P3 | **Codeium** | Popular free-tier alternative to Copilot |
388	| P3 | **Amazon Q** | AWS ecosystem integration |
389	
390	**Validation approach per agent**:
391	1. Install the extension from VS Code Marketplace
392	2. Run a test coding session with a known prompt
393	3. Locate session files: `globalStorage/{id}/`, `~/.{name}/`, workspace dirs
394	4. Document the storage format (JSON, JSONL, custom)
395	5. Implement discoverer + extractor
396	6. Test round-trip: extract → render → verify Markdown
397	7. Add to configuration schema as a toggleable source
398	
399	### Phase 6: Suite Expansion
400	
401	Future helper tools that share infrastructure with the router:
402	
403	| Tool | Purpose |
404	|------|---------|
405	| **Session Handoff Generator** | Auto-generate `SESSION_HANDOFF.md` from recent sessions |
406	| **Transcript Search** | Full-text search across archived sessions |
407	| **Session Diff** | Compare sessions across agents/versions |
408	| **Baseline Reporter** | Generate baseline compliance reports from session data |
409	| **Chat Exporter** | Export current chat panel session directly |
410	
411	---
412	
413	## 4. Technical Details
414	
415	### 4.1 TypeScript Extractor Interface
416	
417	```typescript
418	interface ExtractedSession {
419	  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
420	  messages: SessionMessage[];
421	}
422	
423	interface SessionMessage {
424	  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system' | 'tool' | 'request-prompt';
425	  text: string;
426	  timestamp?: string;
427	  toolCallId?: string;
428	  toolName?: string;
429	}
430	
431	type Extractor = (filePath: string) => ExtractedSession;
432	```
433	
434	### 4.2 Discovery Interface
435	
436	```typescript
437	interface DiscoveredSession {
438	  sourceName: string;      // e.g., "copilot-vscode-windows"
439	  sourceKind: string;      // e.g., "copilot_chat"
440	  filePath: string;        // absolute path to source file
441	  sessionId: string;       // unique session identifier
442	  sizeBytes: number;
443	  mtimeMs: number;
444	}
445	```
446	
447	### 4.3 Renderer Output
448	
449	The renderer must produce Markdown that is **byte-for-byte compatible** with
450	what the Agent Sessions Python `render.py` produces for the same session data.
451	This ensures seamless integration — the Agent Sessions indexer can pick up
452	extension-produced files without modification.
453	
454	### 4.4 Integration with Agent Sessions repo
455	
456	The extension writes Markdown files directly into:
457	```
458	{agentSessionsRepo}/archive/{source_name}/{session_id}.md
459	```
460	
461	The Agent Sessions `archive/index.jsonl` will be updated by the Python tool
462	on its next run, picking up the extension-produced files. Alternatively, the
463	extension could also write a companion index entry.
464	
465	---
466	
467	## 5. Success Criteria
468	
469	1. ✅ Extension discovers DeepSeek sessions from `globalStorage`
470	2. ✅ Extension extracts structured messages from DeepSeek request dumps
471	3. ✅ Extension renders Markdown compatible with Agent Sessions format
472	4. ✅ Extension writes output to configurable directory
473	5. ✅ Copilot Chat sessions are discoverable and extractable
474	6. 🔧 Continue.dev sessions discoverable and extractable (Phase 5)
475	7. 🔧 Cline sessions discoverable and extractable (Phase 5)
476	8. 🔧 Watcher auto-exports new sessions (Phase 3)
477	9. 🔧 At least 3 aggregator-backed agents fully supported (Phase 5)
478	10. 🔧 Agent Sessions Python tool can consume extension-produced Markdown
479	   without modification (verified by `python tools/agent_archive.py export`)
480	11. 🔧 Extension published as private `.vsix` for distribution
481	
482	---
483	
484	## 6. Repository Setup
485	
486	- **Repo name**: `agent-session-router` (or `vscode-agent-tools`)
487	- **Location**: `c:\Users\avidu\Projects\Extensions`
488	- **Visibility**: Private (GitHub)
489	- **Remote**: `https://github.com/avidullu/agent-session-router`
490	- **Monorepo strategy**: This repo hosts the VS Code extension. Future helper
491	  tools can be added as separate packages in a monorepo under `packages/`.
492	
493	---
494	
495	## 7. Timeline Estimate
496	
497	| Phase | Effort | Dependencies |
498	|-------|--------|-------------|
499	| Phase 1: Foundation | ✅ Done | TypeScript, VS Code Extension API |
500	| Phase 2: Copilot Extractor | 3-4 days | Understanding Copilot session format |
501	| Phase 3: Watcher | 1-2 days | chokidar or vscode FileSystemWatcher |
502	| Phase 4: DeepSeek Enhanced | 2-3 days | DeepSeek dump format analysis |
503	| Phase 5: Aggregator Agents | ~1 day/agent | Installing + reverse-engineering each extension's storage |
504	| Phase 6: Suite Expansion | Ongoing | Prior phases complete |
505	
506	---
507	
508	## 8. Open Questions
509	
510	1. **Copilot Chat session format stability**: Is the `debug-logs` / `chat-session-resources`
511	   format stable across VS Code versions, or does it change? Need to test across
512	   Insider/Stable builds.
513	2. **Extension marketplace vs private distribution**: Should this be published
514	   publicly or distributed as a private `.vsix`?
515	3. **Multi-machine sync**: How should the extension handle machines that share
516	   the Agent Sessions repo but have different VS Code storage layouts?
517	4. **Session privacy**: Agent sessions contain file contents, credentials, and
518	   sensitive data. The extension must not accidentally expose these. Markdown
519	   output goes to a private repo only.
520	5. **Aggregator extension storage stability**: Do extensions like Continue/Cline
521	   guarantee stable on-disk formats, or do they change between versions? Need
522	   to pin tested versions and detect format changes.
523	6. **Which aggregator extensions ACTUALLY persist sessions to disk?** Some may
524	   keep everything in-memory (like Z.AI appears to). Need to verify per-extension
525	   before writing discoverers. The table in §2.3 marks known persistence;
526	   agents marked 🔮 need on-machine verification.
527	7. **Ollama/Kimi/Qwen as first-class agents**: If a native VS Code extension
528	   ships for Ollama, Kimi, or Qwen (independent of aggregators like Continue),
529	   they would be Category A agents and follow the standard discoverer+extractor
530	   recipe.
531	8. **Conflict resolution**: If both the Agent Sessions Python tool AND the
532	   VS Code extension export the same session (e.g., Codex via both paths),
533	   the idempotent SHA-256 + size+mtime cache prevents duplication, but the
534	   metadata `source_name` may differ. Should the extension adopt the same
535	   `source_name` convention as the Python tool's `default_sources.toml`?
536

### 56. assistant (2026-07-09T03:52:02.135Z)

Done. The fix is up as a PR and fully verified.

## What was wrong
The repo `agent-session-router` had the VS Code extension nested one level too deep — at `agent-session-router/agent-session-router/`. You had to click into a same-named subfolder to reach the code.

## What I did (on branch `flatten-nested-dir` → PR)
- **Moved all 39 extension files up to the repo root** via `git mv` — GitHub shows them as **renames**, so history is preserved (`src/`, `test/`, `docs/`, `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `README.md`, `SETUP.md`, `.vscodeignore`).
- **Merged the two `.gitignore` files** — the nested one's only unique line was `coverage/`; added it to the root `.gitignore` and removed the duplicate.
- **Fixed `.vscode/launch.json` + `tasks.json`** — they hard-coded the `/agent-session-router` path (`extensionDevelopmentPath`, `outFiles`, npm task `path` + labels). Without this, F5-debug and the build tasks would have broken after the move.
- **No doc changes needed** — `README.md`'s "Project Structure" section already documented the *flat* layout, and the `cd agent-session-router` lines refer to the cloned repo dir (where `package.json` now lives). The docs were written for the flat structure; the repo just never matched them.

## Verification
- `npm install` → clean (176 packages)
- `npm run compile` (`tsc -p ./`) → **exit 0**, `out/extension.js` built, all imports resolve from the new root
- Remote branch tree confirmed flat (no nested dir); PR is **MERGEABLE**

**→ [PR #10: Flatten redundant agent-session-router/ nesting to repo root](https://github.com/avidullu/agent-session-router/pull/10)**

## One heads-up
`PLAN.md` §6 mentions a *future* monorepo strategy (`packages/`), which is probably why it was nested originally. This PR makes it a single-package repo as you asked — if you ever pursue the monorepo, that'd be a separate restructure.

I left the PR for you to merge rather than pushing straight to `master` (matching your PR/review workflow). **Want me to merge it now?** A local clone is at `~/Projects/agent-session-router` on the `flatten-nested-dir` branch if you'd like to poke at it first.

### 62. user (2026-07-09T03:53:39.491Z)

1	# Agent Sessions
2	
3	Private archive for coding-agent sessions across machines and tools.
4	
5	This repo is meant to hold portable Markdown/PDF exports, plus the tooling used
6	to recreate them from local agent stores. Raw logs are supported but ignored by
7	Git by default because they can be large and may contain credentials, file
8	contents, or tool outputs.
9	
10	## Current Importers
11	
12	- Codex Windows: local and archived JSONL sessions
13	- Claude Code Windows/WSL: project JSONL sessions
14	- Gemini Antigravity Windows: transcript JSONL files
15	- Grok WSL: `chat_history.jsonl` files
16	- DeepSeek V4 VS Code extension: request dump prompts from VS Code globalStorage
17	
18	VS Code Copilot Chat and Z.AI/ZAI locations are discovered and documented, but
19	the first exporter treats them as source inventory unless transcript files are
20	present in known locations.
21	
22	Default sources live in `config/default_sources.toml`. Copy
23	`sources.example.toml` to `sources.toml` for local machine overrides.
24	
25	## Quick Start
26	
27	```powershell
28	python -m pip install -e ".[dev]"
29	python .\tools\agent_archive.py discover --write docs\DISCOVERY.md
30	python .\tools\agent_archive.py status
31	python .\tools\agent_archive.py export --all
32	```
33	
34	Optional PDF output:
35	
36	```powershell
37	python .\tools\agent_archive.py export --all --pdf
38	```
39	
40	Optional raw backups:
41	
42	```powershell
43	python .\tools\agent_archive.py export --all --copy-raw
44	```
45	
46	Raw files land under `raw/`, which is ignored by Git unless you intentionally
47	force-add it.
48	
49	### Expected Outputs
50	
51	`discover --write docs\DISCOVERY.md` refreshes the local source inventory:
52	
53	- configured source roots, whether each root exists, and matching file counts;
54	- sample files per source;
55	- inventory-only sources such as Copilot/ZAI storage locations, even when they
56	  are not exportable transcript sources yet.
57	
58	`status` prints archive freshness and convergence signals:
59	
60	- indexed records, visible configured files, new files, and changed files;
61	- indexed records not visible from this machine, preserved from other machines;
62	- source counts and inferred origin environments.
63	
64	`export --all` writes Markdown archive artifacts and updates the shared catalog:
65	
66	- `archive/**/*.md`
67	- `archive/index.jsonl`
68	- `archive/INDEX.md`
69	
70	`export --all --pdf` also writes `archive/**/*.pdf` when `reportlab` is
71	installed. The `.[dev]` install includes `reportlab`; to check a minimal
72	environment, run:
73	
74	```powershell
75	python -c "import reportlab; print('reportlab ok')"
76	```
77	
78	If PDF support is missing, Markdown export still works. The CLI will report that
79	PDF export requires `reportlab`.
80	
81	Some configured sources are intentionally inventory-only. A message like this is
82	expected unless transcript files exist in supported locations:
83	
84	```text
85	Skipped sources without extractors:
86	- copilot-vscode-windows-inventory (inventory)
87	- copilot-vscode-wsl-ubuntu-inventory (inventory)
88	- zai-vscode-wsl-ubuntu-inventory (inventory)
89	```
90

### 63. user (2026-07-09T03:53:40.548Z)

1	# Copy this file to sources.toml for machine-specific overrides.
2	# sources.toml is ignored by Git.
3	
4	[archive]
5	archive_dir = "archive"
6	raw_dir = "raw"
7

### 68. user (2026-07-09T03:58:05.802Z)

1	/**
2	 * Markdown renderer — produces output compatible with the Agent Sessions
3	 * Python render.py format so the archive pipeline can consume files
4	 * produced by this extension without modification.
5	 *
6	 * Output format:
7	 *   # {source_name} / {session_id}
8	 *   ## Metadata
9	 *   - Source: `{source_name}`
10	 *   - Kind: `{source_kind}`
11	 *   - Source file: `{filePath}`
12	 *   - SHA-256: `{digest}`
13	 *   - Source modified: `{mtime_iso}`
14	 *   - Imported at: `{imported_at}`
15	 *   ## Transcript
16	 *   ### 1. {role} ({timestamp})
17	 *   {message text}
18	 */
19	
20	import { ExtractedSession } from '../types';
21	import { isoNow, toPosixPath } from '../utils';
22	
23	export interface RenderContext {
24	    sourceName: string;
25	    sourceKind: string;
26	    sourceFilePath: string;
27	    digest: string;
28	    sourceModifiedAt: string;
29	    importedAt?: string;
30	}
31	
32	export function renderMarkdown(session: ExtractedSession, ctx: RenderContext): string {
33	    const title = `${ctx.sourceName} / ${session.metadata.session_id || 'unknown'}`;
34	    const importedAt = ctx.importedAt || isoNow();
35	
36	    const lines: string[] = [
37	        `# ${title}`,
38	        '',
39	        '## Metadata',
40	        '',
41	        `- Source: \`${ctx.sourceName}\``,
42	        `- Kind: \`${ctx.sourceKind}\``,
43	        `- Source file: \`${toPosixPath(ctx.sourceFilePath)}\``,
44	        `- SHA-256: \`${ctx.digest}\``,
45	        `- Source modified: \`${ctx.sourceModifiedAt}\``,
46	        `- Imported at: \`${importedAt}\``,
47	    ];
48	
49	    // Emit extra metadata keys
50	    for (const key of Object.keys(session.metadata).sort()) {
51	        const value = session.metadata[key];
52	        if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') continue;
53	        if (key === 'session_id' || key === 'source_file') continue; // already covered
54	        lines.push(`- ${key}: \`${String(value)}\``);
55	    }
56	
57	    lines.push('', '## Transcript', '');
58	
59	    if (session.messages.length === 0) {
60	        lines.push('_No transcript messages were extracted from this file._');
61	        return lines.join('\n') + '\n';
62	    }
63	
64	    for (let i = 0; i < session.messages.length; i++) {
65	        const msg = session.messages[i];
66	        let heading = `### ${i + 1}. ${msg.role || 'message'}`;
67	        if (msg.timestamp) {
68	            heading += ` (${msg.timestamp})`;
69	        }
70	        if (msg.toolName) {
71	            heading += ` [tool: ${msg.toolName}]`;
72	        }
73	        lines.push(heading, '', msg.text.trimEnd(), '');
74	    }
75	
76	    return lines.join('\n').trimEnd() + '\n';
77	}
78

1	/**
2	 * Shared types for agent session routing.
3	 */
4	
5	export interface SessionMessage {
6	    /** Role: 'user', 'assistant', 'system', 'tool', 'request-prompt' */
7	    role: string;
8	    /** Message text content */
9	    text: string;
10	    /** ISO-8601 timestamp if available */
11	    timestamp?: string;
12	    /** Tool call ID (for tool role messages) */
13	    toolCallId?: string;
14	    /** Tool name (for tool role messages) */
15	    toolName?: string;
16	}
17	
18	export interface ExtractedSession {
19	    /** Arbitrary metadata key-value pairs */
20	    metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
21	    /** Ordered list of conversation messages */
22	    messages: SessionMessage[];
23	}
24	
25	export interface DiscoveredSession {
26	    /** Source name (e.g., "copilot-vscode-windows") */
27	    sourceName: string;
28	    /** Source kind (e.g., "copilot_chat") */
29	    sourceKind: string;
30	    /** Absolute path to the source file */
31	    filePath: string;
32	    /** Unique session identifier */
33	    sessionId: string;
34	    /** File size in bytes */
35	    sizeBytes: number;
36	    /** Last modified time in milliseconds since epoch */
37	    mtimeMs: number;
38	}
39	
40	/**
41	 * An extractor function: takes a file path, returns an ExtractedSession.
42	 */
43	export type Extractor = (filePath: string) => ExtractedSession;
44	
45	/**
46	 * A discoverer function: returns an async iterable of DiscoveredSession.
47	 */
48	export type Discoverer = () => AsyncIterable<DiscoveredSession>;
49	
50	export interface ExportRecord {
51	    sourceName: string;
52	    sourceKind: string;
53	    filePath: string;
54	    sessionId: string;
55	    digest: string;
56	    sizeBytes: number;
57	    mtimeMs: number;
58	    markdownPath: string;
59	    exportedAt: string;
60	}

1	"""Markdown and PDF rendering."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import datetime as dt
6	import re
7	import textwrap
8	from pathlib import Path
9	
10	from .models import ExtractedSession, Source
11	from .utils import now_utc
12	
13	
14	def markdown_for_session(
15	    source: Source,
16	    path: Path,
17	    session: ExtractedSession,
18	    digest: str,
19	    imported_at: str | None = None,
20	) -> str:
21	    title_bits = [source.name, str(session.metadata.get("session_id") or path.stem)]
22	    title = " / ".join(x for x in title_bits if x)
23	    lines = [
24	        f"# {title}",
25	        "",
26	        "## Metadata",
27	        "",
28	        f"- Source: `{source.name}`",
29	        f"- Kind: `{source.kind}`",
30	        f"- Source file: `{path}`",
31	        f"- SHA-256: `{digest}`",
32	        f"- Source modified: `{modified_timestamp(path)}`",
33	        f"- Imported at: `{imported_at or now_utc()}`",
34	    ]
35	    for key in sorted(session.metadata):
36	        value = session.metadata[key]
37	        if value in (None, ""):
38	            continue
39	        lines.append(f"- {key}: `{value}`")
40	    lines.extend(["", "## Transcript", ""])
41	    if not session.messages:
42	        lines.append("_No transcript messages were extracted from this file._")
43	        return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
44	    for idx, msg in enumerate(session.messages, 1):
45	        heading = f"### {idx}. {msg.role or 'message'}"
46	        if msg.timestamp:
47	            heading += f" ({msg.timestamp})"
48	        lines.extend([heading, "", msg.text.rstrip(), ""])
49	    return "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
50	
51	
52	def modified_timestamp(path: Path) -> str:
53	    return dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(path.stat().st_mtime, dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
54	
55	
56	def write_pdf(markdown: str, out_path: Path) -> bool:
57	    try:
58	        from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import letter
59	        from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas
60	    except Exception:
61	        return False
62	
63	    ansi = re.compile(r"\x1b\[[0-?]*[ -/]*[@-~]")
64	    pdf = canvas.Canvas(str(out_path), pagesize=letter)
65	    width, height = letter
66	    margin = 42
67	    y = height - margin
68	    line_height = 11
69	    max_chars = 96
70	
71	    def emit(line: str, font: str = "Courier", size: int = 8) -> None:
72	        nonlocal y
73	        if y < margin:
74	            pdf.showPage()
75	            y = height - margin
76	        pdf.setFont(font, size)
77	        safe = ansi.sub("", line).encode("latin-1", "replace").decode("latin-1")
78	        pdf.drawString(margin, y, safe)
79	        y -= line_height
80	
81	    for raw in markdown.splitlines():
82	        line = raw.rstrip()
83	        if line.startswith("# "):
84	            y -= 4
85	            emit(line[2:], "Helvetica-Bold", 14)
86	            y -= 4
87	            continue
88	        if line.startswith("## "):
89	            y -= 3
90	            emit(line[3:], "Helvetica-Bold", 11)
91	            y -= 2
92	            continue
93	        if line.startswith("### "):
94	            y -= 2
95	            emit(line[4:], "Helvetica-Bold", 9)
96	            continue
97	        if not line:
98	            y -= line_height
99	            continue
100	        for wrapped in textwrap.wrap(line, width=max_chars, replace_whitespace=False) or [""]:
101	            emit(wrapped)
102	    pdf.save()
103	    return True
104

### 73. assistant (2026-07-09T03:59:38.880Z)

This is the decisive comparison, and it reveals the core problem. Let me line up the two renderers:

**Python `render.py` (canonical) vs TypeScript `markdown.ts` (router)** — I can already see **real divergences** that break the "byte-for-byte compatible" claim the router's own docstring makes:

1. **Tool suffix**: TS appends `[tool: {toolName}]` to transcript headings; Python never does.
2. **Metadata bullets**: Python emits *all* metadata keys including `session_id`/`source_file`; TS explicitly *skips* those two.
3. **Title fallback**: Python falls back to `path.stem`; TS falls back to literal `'unknown'`.
4. **Source-file path & timestamp**: Python emits the OS-native path (`str(Path)` → backslashes on Windows) and `isoformat(timespec=seconds)` (`+00:00`); TS forces posix slashes and passes its own timestamp string.

So a conformance test built today would **fail immediately** — which is exactly why you want one. Let me confirm the details against ground truth (an actual archive file) and the router's call site before designing the reconciliation. Setting up tracking first.

1	/**
2	 * Router orchestrator — the central pipeline:
3	 *   1. Discover sessions from configured sources
4	 *   2. Extract structured messages via registered extractors
5	 *   3. Render Markdown
6	 *   4. Write output files to the configured directory
7	 */
8	
9	import * as fs from 'fs';
10	import * as path from 'path';
11	import * as vscode from 'vscode';
12	import { DiscoveredSession, ExportRecord } from './types';
13	import { getDiscoverer } from './discoverers/index';
14	import { getExtractor } from './extractors/index';
15	import { renderMarkdown } from './renderers/markdown';
16	import { sha256File, slugify, isoNow, canReuseRecord } from './utils';
17	import { getConfig, Config } from './config';
18	import {
19	    logDiscover,
20	    logExtractStart,
21	    logExtractResult,
22	    logExtractError,
23	    logRender,
24	    logWrite,
25	    logWriteError,
26	    logSkip,
27	    logExportSummary,
28	} from './logger';
29	
30	// Auto-load all discoverers and extractors from their directories.
31	// Users add new agents by dropping files into these folders — no core edits needed.
32	function autoLoadModules(dir: string): void {
33	    const dirPath = path.join(__dirname, dir);
34	    if (!fs.existsSync(dirPath)) return;
35	    for (const file of fs.readdirSync(dirPath)) {
36	        if (!file.endsWith('.js') || file === 'index.js') continue;
37	        try {
38	            require(path.join(dirPath, file));
39	        } catch (err) {
40	            // Per-file error: one broken plugin must not disable all others.
41	            // Built-in modules (copilot-chat, deepseek) failing is still a
42	            // diagnosable error visible in the Output Channel.
43	            const error = err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err));
44	            logExtractError('autoLoad', path.join(dirPath, file), dir, error);
45	            console.error(
46	                `[agent-session-router] Failed to load ${dir}/${file}: ${error.message}. ` +
47	                `This agent source will be unavailable. Check the Output Channel for details.`
48	            );
49	        }
50	    }
51	}
52	autoLoadModules('discoverers');
53	autoLoadModules('extractors');
54	
55	/** In-memory cache of previous export records (keyed by filePath). */
56	const exportCache = new Map<string, ExportRecord>();
57	
58	export function resetExportCache(): void {
59	    exportCache.clear();
60	}
61	
62	export function resolveOutputDir(config: Config): string {
63	    if (config.outputDir) {
64	        return config.outputDir;
65	    }
66	    // Default: try to find Agent Sessions repo relative to common locations
67	    const candidates = [
68	        path.join(process.env.USERPROFILE || '~', 'Projects', 'Agent Sessions', 'archive'),
69	    ];
70	    for (const candidate of candidates) {
71	        if (fs.existsSync(candidate)) {
72	            return candidate;
73	        }
74	    }
75	    // Fallback to a local staging directory
76	    return path.join(process.env.USERPROFILE || '~', '.agent-sessions-staging');
77	}
78	
79	export async function discoverAllSessions(
80	    progress: vscode.Progress<{ message?: string }>,
81	): Promise<DiscoveredSession[]> {
82	    const config = getConfig();
83	    const allSessions: DiscoveredSession[] = [];
84	
85	    // Discover all registered kinds that are enabled in config.
86	    // Users add new agents by registering a discoverer — config automatically picks it up.
87	    const { knownKinds } = require('./discoverers/index') as { knownKinds: () => string[] };
88	    const kindsToDiscover = knownKinds().filter(
89	        kind => config.sources[kind]?.enabled !== false
90	    );
91	
92	    for (const kind of kindsToDiscover) {
93	        const discoverer = getDiscoverer(kind);
94	        if (!discoverer) continue;
95	
96	        progress.report({ message: `Discovering ${kind} sessions...` });
97	
98	        for await (const session of discoverer()) {
99	            logDiscover(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId, {
100	                sizeBytes: session.sizeBytes,
101	                mtimeMs: session.mtimeMs,
102	            });
103	            allSessions.push(session);
104	        }
105	    }
106	
107	    return allSessions;
108	}
109	
110	export async function exportSession(
111	    session: DiscoveredSession,
112	    outputDir: string,
113	): Promise<ExportRecord | null> {
114	    const extractor = getExtractor(session.sourceKind);
115	    if (!extractor) {
116	        logSkip(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId,
117	            `No extractor registered for kind: ${session.sourceKind}`);
118	        return null;
119	    }
120	
121	    // Check cache for unchanged files
122	    const cached = exportCache.get(session.filePath) ?? null;
123	    if (cached && canReuseRecord(cached, session.sizeBytes, session.mtimeMs)) {
124	        logSkip(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId,
125	            'Unchanged since last export (cached)');
126	        return cached;
127	    }
128	
129	    // Extract
130	    const extractDone = logExtractStart(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId, session.sizeBytes);
131	    let extracted;
132	    try {
133	        extracted = extractor(session.filePath);
134	    } catch (err) {
135	        extractDone();
136	        const error = err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err));
137	        let snippet: string | undefined;
138	        try {
139	            snippet = fs.readFileSync(session.filePath, 'utf-8').slice(0, 500);
140	        } catch { /* file may not be readable */ }
141	        logExtractError(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId, error, snippet);
142	        return null;
143	    }
144	    extractDone();
145	
146	    if (!extracted.messages.length) {
147	        logSkip(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId,
148	            'No messages extracted (empty session)');
149	        return null;
150	    }
151	
152	    logExtractResult(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId,
153	        extracted.messages.length, 0);
154	
155	    // Compute digest
156	    let digest: string;
157	    try {
158	        digest = sha256File(session.filePath);
159	    } catch (err) {
160	        const error = err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err));
161	        logExtractError(session.sourceKind, session.filePath, session.sessionId, error);
162	        return null;
163	    }
164	
165	    // Determine output path
166	    const sourceDir = slugify(session.sourceName);
167	    const sessionFile = slugify(String(extracted.metadata.session_id || path.basename(session.filePath, path.extname(session.filePath))));
168	    const markdownPath = path.join(outputDir, sourceDir, `${sessionFile}.md`);
169	
170	    // Render
171	    const modifiedDate = new Date(session.mtimeMs);
172	    const markdown = renderMarkdown(extracted, {
173	        sourceName: session.sourceName,
174	        sourceKind: session.sourceKind,
175	        sourceFilePath: session.filePath,
176	        digest,
177	        sourceModifiedAt: modifiedDate.toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, 'Z'),
178	    });
179	
180	    logRender(session.sourceKind, session.sessionId, markdownPath, extracted.messages.length);
181	
182	    // Write
183	    const dir = path.dirname(markdownPath);
184	    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
185	        fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
186	    }
187	    try {
188	        fs.writeFileSync(markdownPath, markdown, 'utf-8');
189	        logWrite(session.sourceKind, session.sessionId, markdownPath, Buffer.byteLength(markdown, 'utf-8'));
190	    } catch (err) {
191	        const error = err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err));
192	        logWriteError(session.sourceKind, session.sessionId, markdownPath, error);
193	        return null;
194	    }
195	
196	    const record: ExportRecord = {
197	        sourceName: session.sourceName,
198	        sourceKind: session.sourceKind,
199	        filePath: session.filePath,
200	        sessionId: session.sessionId,
201	        digest,
202	        sizeBytes: session.sizeBytes,
203	        mtimeMs: session.mtimeMs,
204	        markdownPath,
205	        exportedAt: isoNow(),
206	    };
207	
208	    exportCache.set(session.filePath, record);
209	    return record;
210	}
211	
212	export async function exportAllSessions(
213	    progress: vscode.Progress<{ message?: string; increment?: number }>,
214	): Promise<ExportRecord[]> {
215	    const config = getConfig();
216	    const outputDir = resolveOutputDir(config);
217	
218	    progress.report({ message: 'Discovering sessions...' });
219	    const sessions = await discoverAllSessions(progress);
220	
221	    if (sessions.length === 0) {
222	        vscode.window.showInformationMessage('Agent Session Router: No sessions found to export.');
223	        return [];
224	    }
225	
226	    const records: ExportRecord[] = [];
227	    const total = sessions.length;
228	    let completed = 0;
229	    let exported = 0;
230	    let skipped = 0;
231	    let failed = 0;
232	    const startTime = Date.now();
233	
234	    for (const session of sessions) {
235	        progress.report({
236	            message: `Exporting ${completed + 1}/${total}: ${session.sourceName}`,
237	            increment: (100 / total),
238	        });
239	
240	        const record = await exportSession(session, outputDir);
241	        if (record) {
242	            records.push(record);
243	            exported++;
244	        } else {
245	            // Check if it was skipped (cached) or failed
246	            // The logger already recorded the reason
247	            failed++;
248	        }
249	        completed++;
250	    }
251	
252	    const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;
253	    logExportSummary(total, exported, skipped, failed, durationMs);
254	
255	    return records;
256	}
257

1	/**
2	 * Shared utilities: hashing, path resolution, timestamps.
3	 */
4	
5	import * as crypto from 'crypto';
6	import * as fs from 'fs';
7	import * as path from 'path';
8	import * as os from 'os';
9	
10	/** Compute SHA-256 hex digest of a file. */
11	export function sha256File(filePath: string): string {
12	    const h = crypto.createHash('sha256');
13	    const data = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
14	    h.update(data);
15	    return h.digest('hex');
16	}
17	
18	/** Get file stat (size + mtime). */
19	export function fileStat(filePath: string): { size: number; mtimeMs: number } {
20	    const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
21	    return { size: stat.size, mtimeMs: stat.mtimeMs };
22	}
23	
24	/** Resolve a path that may contain ~ or environment variables. */
25	export function resolvePath(raw: string): string {
26	    let resolved = raw;
27	    if (resolved.startsWith('~')) {
28	        resolved = path.join(os.homedir(), resolved.slice(1));
29	    }
30	    // Expand %VAR% style on Windows
31	    resolved = resolved.replace(/%([^%]+)%/g, (_match, name) => process.env[name] || '');
32	    return path.resolve(resolved);
33	}
34	
35	/** Generate an ISO-8601 UTC timestamp string. */
36	export function isoNow(): string {
37	    return new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, 'Z');
38	}
39	
40	/** Sanitize a string for use as a filename segment. */
41	export function slugify(text: string): string {
42	    return text
43	        .replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_')
44	        .replace(/\s+/g, '-')
45	        .slice(0, 200);
46	}
47	
48	/** Convert a Windows file path to a POSIX-style relative path. */
49	export function toPosixPath(p: string): string {
50	    return p.replace(/\\/g, '/');
51	}
52	
53	/**
54	 * Check whether a cached export record is still valid
55	 * (same file size and mtime as when last exported).
56	 */
57	export function canReuseRecord(
58	    prior: { sizeBytes: number; mtimeMs: number } | undefined,
59	    currentSize: number,
60	    currentMtimeMs: number,
61	): boolean {
62	    if (!prior) return false;
63	    return prior.sizeBytes === currentSize && prior.mtimeMs === currentMtimeMs;
64	}
65

### 83. assistant (2026-07-09T04:00:51.671Z)

Ground truth confirms the divergences — and reveals more. The actual archive file shows:
- `- Source file:` uses **native Windows backslashes** (router forces posix `/`)
- timestamps use **`+00:00`** seconds format (router emits **`Z`**)
- `- session_id:` **is** emitted as a metadata bullet (router skips it)
- Router's `router.ts:167-168` names files `{slug(session_id)}.md` with **no date prefix / hash suffix**, but the archive uses `20260702-{id}-{stem}-{hash}.md` — and the router **never updates `index.jsonl`/`INDEX.md`**.

So the two are materially **not** compatible today. I need `archive.py` (filename + index scheme) and `models.py` to finish mapping the contract before designing the fix.

1	"""Archive export, discovery, and index operations."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import datetime as dt
6	import gzip
7	import hashlib
8	import json
9	import os
10	import re
11	import shutil
12	import sys
13	from dataclasses import dataclass
14	from pathlib import Path
15	from typing import Any, Iterable
16	
17	from .config import ArchiveConfig
18	from .models import Source
19	from .render import markdown_for_session, write_pdf
20	from .sources.registry import get_extractor
21	from .utils import now_utc, read_jsonl_dicts, slugify
22	
23	
24	IMPORTED_AT_RE = re.compile(r"^- Imported at: `([^`]+)`$", re.MULTILINE)
25	GENERATED_RE = re.compile(r"^Generated: `([^`]+)`$", re.MULTILINE)
26	ROUTER_INDEX_FILENAME = ".router-index.jsonl"
27	
28	
29	@dataclass(frozen=True)
30	class ExportResult:
31	    exported: int
32	    pdf_missing: bool = False
33	    skipped_sources: tuple[str, ...] = ()
34	
35	
36	def iter_source_files(source: Source) -> Iterable[Path]:
37	    seen: set[Path] = set()
38	    for root in source.roots:
39	        if not root.exists():
40	            continue
41	        for path in root.glob(source.glob):
42	            if not path.is_file():
43	                continue
44	            resolved = path.resolve()
45	            if resolved in seen:
46	                continue
47	            seen.add(resolved)
48	            yield path
49	
50	
51	def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
52	    h = hashlib.sha256()
53	    with path.open("rb") as f:
54	        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
55	            h.update(chunk)
56	    return h.hexdigest()
57	
58	
59	def copy_raw(config: ArchiveConfig, path: Path, source: Source, digest: str) -> Path:
60	    raw_target = config.raw_dir / source.name / f"{digest[:16]}-{path.name}.gz"
61	    raw_target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
62	    with path.open("rb") as src, gzip.open(raw_target, "wb") as dst:
63	        shutil.copyfileobj(src, dst)
64	    return raw_target
65	
66	
67	def select_sources(config: ArchiveConfig, selected: list[str] | None) -> list[Source]:
68	    if not selected:
69	        return list(config.sources)
70	    wanted = set(selected)
71	    known = {source.name for source in config.sources} | {source.kind for source in config.sources}
72	    for selector in sorted(wanted - known):
73	        print(f"warning: --source {selector!r} matched no configured source name or kind.", file=sys.stderr)
74	    return [source for source in config.sources if source.name in wanted or source.kind in wanted]
75	
76	
77	def _index_path_exists(config: ArchiveConfig, rel: Any) -> bool:
78	    return isinstance(rel, str) and bool(rel) and (config.repo_root / rel.replace("\\", "/")).exists()
79	
80	
81	def _can_reuse_record(
82	    config: ArchiveConfig,
83	    prior: dict[str, Any] | None,
84	    size: int,
85	    mtime: float,
86	    write_pdfs: bool,
87	    copy_raw_files: bool,
88	) -> bool:
89	    """Skip re-hashing/re-extracting a source file that is unchanged since the
90	    last export (matching size + mtime) as long as the outputs it would produce
91	    already exist on disk. Records written before TD15 lack size/mtime and never
92	    match, so they fall through to a full re-export.
93	
94	    Tradeoff: identity is (size, mtime), not a content hash — a same-size,
95	    same-mtime in-place edit (unusual for append-only agent logs) would be
96	    missed. Run `export` after touching a file, or `prune`/re-export, to force a
97	    fresh hash if that ever matters."""
98	    if prior is None or prior.get("size") != size or prior.get("mtime") != mtime:
99	        return False
100	    if not _index_path_exists(config, prior.get("markdown")):
101	        return False
102	    if write_pdfs and not _index_path_exists(config, prior.get("pdf")):
103	        return False
104	    if copy_raw_files and not _index_path_exists(config, prior.get("raw")):
105	        return False
106	    return True
107	
108	
109	def export_sources(
110	    config: ArchiveConfig,
111	    selected: list[str] | None = None,
112	    limit: int | None = None,
113	    write_pdfs: bool = False,
114	    copy_raw_files: bool = False,
115	    dry_run: bool = False,
116	) -> ExportResult:
117	    sources = select_sources(config, selected)
118	    config.archive_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
119	    existing_records = read_existing_index_records(config)
120	    prior_by_key = {} if dry_run else {index_record_key(record): record for record in existing_records}
121	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
122	    pdf_missing = False
123	    skipped_sources: list[str] = []
124	    exported = 0
125	
126	    for source in sources:
127	        extractor = get_extractor(source.kind)
128	        if extractor is None:
129	            skipped_sources.append(f"{source.name} ({source.kind})")
130	            continue
131	        for root in source.roots:
132	            if "__missing_" in str(root):
133	                print(
134	                    f"warning: source {source.name!r} has an unresolved path template "
135	                    f"({root}); skipping that root. Set it in sources.toml if this machine has it.",
136	                    file=sys.stderr,
137	                )
138	        for path in iter_source_files(source):
139	            if limit and exported >= limit:
140	                break
141	            stat_result = path.stat()
142	            size, mtime = stat_result.st_size, stat_result.st_mtime
143	            prior = prior_by_key.get((source.name, str(path)))
144	            if _can_reuse_record(config, prior, size, mtime, write_pdfs, copy_raw_files):
145	                assert prior is not None  # _can_reuse_record returns False for None
146	                records.append(prior)  # unchanged since last export: skip hashing/extraction/render
147	                exported += 1
148	                continue
149	            digest = sha256_file(path)
150	            session = extractor(path)
151	            session_id = str(session.metadata.get("session_id") or path.stem)
152	            changed = source_modified_date(path)
153	            stem = slugify(f"{changed}-{session_id}-{path.stem}-{digest[:12]}")
154	            out_dir = config.archive_dir / source.name
155	            out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
156	            md_path = out_dir / f"{stem}.md"
157	            markdown = markdown_for_session(source, path, session, digest, imported_at=existing_imported_at(md_path))
158	            md_changed = True
159	            if not dry_run:
160	                md_changed = write_text_if_changed(md_path, markdown)
161	            pdf_path = None
162	            if write_pdfs:
163	                pdf_path = md_path.with_suffix(".pdf")
164	                should_write_pdf = md_changed or not pdf_path.exists()
165	                if not dry_run and should_write_pdf and not write_pdf(markdown, pdf_path):
166	                    pdf_missing = True
167	                    pdf_path = None
168	            raw_path = None
169	            if copy_raw_files and not dry_run:
170	                raw_path = copy_raw(config, path, source, digest)
171	            records.append(
172	                {
173	                    "source": source.name,
174	                    "kind": source.kind,
175	                    "source_file": str(path),
176	                    "sha256": digest,
177	                    "size": size,
178	                    "mtime": mtime,
179	                    "messages": len(session.messages),
180	                    "markdown": as_repo_relative(config, md_path),
181	                    "pdf": as_repo_relative(config, pdf_path) if pdf_path else None,
182	                    "raw": as_repo_relative(config, raw_path) if raw_path else None,
183	                    "metadata": session.metadata,
184	                }
185	            )
186	            exported += 1
187	        if limit and exported >= limit:
188	            break
189	
190	    if not dry_run:
191	        router_records = read_router_index_records(config)
192	        if router_records:
193	            records = merge_index_records(records, router_records)
194	        write_indexes(config, merge_index_records(existing_records, records))
195	    return ExportResult(exported=exported, pdf_missing=pdf_missing, skipped_sources=tuple(skipped_sources))
196	
197	
198	def index_record_key(record: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str]:
199	    # Path identity: used by `status` to match index records against the files
200	    # visible on THIS machine (keyed by source name + local path).
201	    return (str(record.get("source", "")), str(record.get("source_file", "")))
202	
203	
204	def index_identity_key(record: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
205	    # Merge identity: machine-independent so the same logical session exported
206	    # from Windows and WSL (different absolute source_file paths) collapses to a
207	    # single record, while a re-export of a changed file still supersedes the old
208	    # record instead of accumulating. session_id is stable across both machines
209	    # and content changes; fall back to (source, source_file) when it is absent.
210	    metadata = record.get("metadata")
211	    if isinstance(metadata, dict):
212	        session_id = str(metadata.get("session_id", "")).strip()
213	        if session_id:
214	            return ("session", session_id)
215	    return ("path", str(record.get("source", "")), str(record.get("source_file", "")))
216	
217	
218	def _posix_index_record(record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
219	    # Repo-relative archive paths are written POSIX-normalized once, here, so
220	    # downstream consumers never have to compensate for OS separators.
221	    normalized = dict(record)
222	    for key in ("markdown", "pdf", "raw"):
223	        value = normalized.get(key)
224	        if isinstance(value, str):
225	            normalized[key] = value.replace("\\", "/")
226	    return normalized
227	
228	
229	def read_existing_index_records(config: ArchiveConfig) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
230	    index_path = config.archive_dir / "index.jsonl"
231	    if not index_path.exists():
232	        return []
233	    return read_jsonl_dicts(index_path, label="archive/index.jsonl")
234	
235	
236	def read_router_index_records(config: ArchiveConfig) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
237	    """Read records produced by the agent-session-router VS Code extension.
238	
239	    The extension writes ``archive/.router-index.jsonl`` alongside its rendered
240	    Markdown files. This function reads those records so they can be merged into
241	    the main ``archive/index.jsonl`` without requiring a full re-extraction.
242	
243	    Returns an empty list if the file does not exist or is malformed.
244	    """
245	    router_index_path = config.archive_dir / ROUTER_INDEX_FILENAME
246	    if not router_index_path.exists():
247	        return []
248	    try:
249	        return read_jsonl_dicts(router_index_path, label=f"archive/{ROUTER_INDEX_FILENAME}")
250	    except Exception:
251	        print(f"warning: failed to read {router_index_path}; skipping router index records.", file=sys.stderr)
252	        return []
253	
254	
255	def merge_index_records(existing: list[dict[str, Any]], current: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
256	    merged: dict[tuple[str, ...], dict[str, Any]] = {}
257	    order: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
258	    for record in [*existing, *current]:
259	        key = index_identity_key(record)
260	        if key not in merged:
261	            order.append(key)
262	        merged[key] = record
263	    return [merged[key] for key in order]
264	
265	
266	def write_indexes(config: ArchiveConfig, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
267	    records = [_posix_index_record(record) for record in records]
268	    jsonl_path = config.archive_dir / "index.jsonl"
269	    jsonl_text = "".join(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for record in records)
270	    write_text_if_changed(jsonl_path, jsonl_text)
271	
272	    lines = [
273	        "# Agent Session Archive",
274	        "",
275	        f"Generated: `{now_utc()}`",
276	        "",
277	        "| Source | Kind | Messages | Markdown | PDF |",
278	        "| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |",
279	    ]
280	    for record in records:
281	        md = record["markdown"].replace("\\", "/")
282	        link = os.path.relpath(config.repo_root / md, config.archive_dir).replace("\\", "/")
283	        pdf_link = ""
284	        if record.get("pdf"):
285	            pdf_norm = record["pdf"].replace("\\", "/")
286	            pdf_rel = os.path.relpath(config.repo_root / pdf_norm, config.archive_dir).replace("\\", "/")
287	            pdf_link = f"[PDF]({pdf_rel})"
288	        lines.append(
289	            f"| {record['source']} | {record['kind']} | {record['messages']} | "
290	            f"[{Path(md).name}]({link}) | {pdf_link} |"
291	        )
292	    index_path = config.archive_dir / "INDEX.md"
293	    index_text = preserve_generated_at(index_path, "\n".join(lines) + "\n")
294	    write_text_if_changed(index_path, index_text)
295	
296	
297	def existing_imported_at(path: Path) -> str | None:
298	    if not path.exists():
299	        return None
300	    match = IMPORTED_AT_RE.search(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
301	    return match.group(1) if match else None
302	
303	
304	def preserve_generated_at(path: Path, text: str) -> str:
305	    if not path.exists():
306	        return text
307	    existing = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
308	    if GENERATED_RE.sub("Generated: `<generated>`", existing) != GENERATED_RE.sub("Generated: `<generated>`", text):
309	        return text
310	    match = GENERATED_RE.search(existing)
311	    if not match:
312	        return text
313	    return GENERATED_RE.sub(f"Generated: `{match.group(1)}`", text, count=1)
314	
315	
316	def write_text_if_changed(path: Path, text: str) -> bool:
317	    if path.exists() and path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") == text:
318	        return False
319	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
320	    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
321	    return True
322	
323	
324	def load_index_records(config: ArchiveConfig) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
325	    index_path = config.archive_dir / "index.jsonl"
326	    if not index_path.exists():
327	        raise SystemExit("archive/index.jsonl does not exist. Run export first.")
328	    return read_jsonl_dicts(index_path, label="archive/index.jsonl")
329	
330	
331	def prune_index_records(config: ArchiveConfig, dry_run: bool = False) -> int:
332	    """Drop index records whose archive Markdown file no longer exists on disk.
333	
334	    Safe GC for entries orphaned when a source file's digest changed (a fresh
335	    stem/record supersedes the old one) or an archive file was deleted. Records
336	    from other machines stay put as long as their committed Markdown is present.
337	    """
338	    records = read_existing_index_records(config)
339	    kept: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
340	    dropped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
341	    for record in records:
342	        markdown = record.get("markdown")
343	        target = config.repo_root / str(markdown).replace("\\", "/") if isinstance(markdown, str) else None
344	        if target is not None and target.exists():
345	            kept.append(record)
346	        else:
347	            dropped.append(record)
348	    for record in dropped:
349	        print(f"prune: dropping stale index record for {record.get('markdown')!r} (missing on disk)")
350	    if not dropped:
351	        print("prune: no stale index records found.")
352	    elif not dry_run:
353	        write_indexes(config, kept)
354	        print(f"prune: removed {len(dropped)} record(s); {len(kept)} remain.")
355	    else:
356	        print(f"prune: would remove {len(dropped)} record(s); {len(kept)} would remain.")
357	    return 0
358	
359	
360	def pdf_existing(
361	    config: ArchiveConfig,
362	    selected: list[str] | None = None,
363	    limit: int | None = None,
364	    force: bool = False,
365	) -> int:
366	    records = load_index_records(config)
367	    wanted = set(selected or [])
368	    made = 0
369	    skipped = 0
370	    missing_renderer = False
371	
372	    for record in records:
373	        if wanted and record.get("source") not in wanted and record.get("kind") not in wanted:
374	            continue
375	        if limit and made >= limit:
376	            break
377	        md_path = config.repo_root / record["markdown"]
378	        if not md_path.exists():
379	            skipped += 1
380	            continue
381	        pdf_path = md_path.with_suffix(".pdf")
382	        if pdf_path.exists() and not force:
383	            record["pdf"] = as_repo_relative(config, pdf_path)
384	            skipped += 1
385	            continue
386	        markdown = md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
387	        if not write_pdf(markdown, pdf_path):
388	            missing_renderer = True
389	            break
390	        record["pdf"] = as_repo_relative(config, pdf_path)
391	        made += 1
392	
393	    write_indexes(config, records)
394	    print(f"PDFs written: {made}; skipped: {skipped}.")
395	    if missing_renderer:
396	        print("PDF export requires reportlab. Run: python -m pip install reportlab")
397	        return 1
398	    return 0
399	
400	
401	def discover_sources(config: ArchiveConfig, samples: int = 10, write: str | None = None) -> int:
402	    lines = ["# Agent Session Discovery", "", f"Generated: `{now_utc()}`", ""]
403	    for source in config.sources:
404	        lines.extend([f"## {source.name}", "", f"- Kind: `{source.kind}`", f"- Glob: `{source.glob}`"])
405	        if source.description:
406	            lines.append(f"- Description: {source.description}")
407	        total_files = 0
408	        total_bytes = 0
409	        for root in source.roots:
410	            exists = root.exists()
411	            lines.append(f"- Root: `{root}` ({'exists' if exists else 'missing'})")
412	            if not exists:
413	                continue
414	            files = [p for p in root.glob(source.glob) if p.is_file()]
415	            total_files += len(files)
416	            total_bytes += sum(p.stat().st_size for p in files)
417	        lines.extend([f"- Matching files: `{total_files}`", f"- Matching bytes: `{total_bytes}`", ""])
418	        sample_count = 0
419	        for path in iter_source_files(source):
420	            lines.append(f"  - `{path}` ({path.stat().st_size} bytes)")
421	            sample_count += 1
422	            if sample_count >= samples:
423	                break
424	        lines.append("")
425	
426	    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
427	    if write:
428	        target = Path(write)
429	        if not target.is_absolute():
430	            target = config.repo_root / target
431	        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
432	        target.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
433	    else:
434	        print(text)
435	    return 0
436	
437	
438	def source_modified_date(path: Path) -> str:
439	    return dt_from_timestamp(path.stat().st_mtime)
440	
441	
442	def dt_from_timestamp(timestamp: float) -> str:
443	    # Use UTC so the archive stem is stable regardless of the exporting
444	    # machine's timezone; render.py records the modified timestamp in UTC too,
445	    # so a session near midnight (or exported from two timezones) resolves to a
446	    # single stem and a single archive file.
447	    return dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(timestamp, dt.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d")
448	
449	
450	def as_repo_relative(config: ArchiveConfig, path: Path | None) -> str | None:
451	    if path is None:
452	        return None
453	    return str(path.relative_to(config.repo_root))
454

### 87. assistant (2026-07-09T04:02:08.871Z)

Major discovery: **agent-sessions already has a designed integration seam for the router** that isn't documented anywhere user-facing. In `archive.py`:

```python
ROUTER_INDEX_FILENAME = ".router-index.jsonl"

def read_router_index_records(config):
    """Read records produced by the agent-session-router VS Code extension.
    The extension writes archive/.router-index.jsonl alongside its rendered
    Markdown files... merged into the main archive/index.jsonl..."""
```

And `export_sources` merges those records in. So the Python hub *expects* the router to write `archive/.router-index.jsonl` (in a specific record schema: `source`, `kind`, `source_file`, `sha256`, `size`, `mtime`, `messages`, `markdown` [repo-relative posix], `metadata.session_id`) plus a `.md` whose body matches `markdown_for_session`. The filename stem is `slugify("{YYYYMMDD}-{session_id}-{path.stem}-{digest[:12]}")`.

The reconciliation is now concrete: make the router **honor a contract agent-sessions already implements**. I need to confirm whether the router writes that index today, and nail down `utils.py` (`slugify`, `now_utc`) so the router can match it, plus find existing tests/CI.

1	"""Small utility functions shared across archive modules."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import datetime as dt
6	import json
7	import re
8	import sys
9	from pathlib import Path
10	from typing import Any, Iterable
11	
12	
13	def now_utc() -> str:
14	    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
15	
16	
17	def read_jsonl_dicts(path: Path, *, label: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
18	    """Read a JSONL file into dicts, warning on (and skipping) malformed lines.
19	
20	    The single tolerant reader shared by every JSONL consumer so one corrupt
21	    line degrades to a warning instead of a raw ``JSONDecodeError`` traceback in
22	    one place and a silent skip in another.
23	    """
24	    tag = label or str(path)
25	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
26	    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
27	        for lineno, raw in enumerate(f, start=1):
28	            line = raw.strip()
29	            if not line:
30	                continue
31	            try:
32	                obj = json.loads(line)
33	            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
34	                print(f"warning: skipping malformed JSON at {tag}:{lineno}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
35	                continue
36	            if isinstance(obj, dict):
37	                records.append(obj)
38	    return records
39	
40	
41	def jsonl_objects(path: Path) -> Iterable[dict[str, Any]]:
42	    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
43	        for line in f:
44	            line = line.strip()
45	            if not line:
46	                continue
47	            try:
48	                obj = json.loads(line)
49	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
50	                continue
51	            if isinstance(obj, dict):
52	                yield obj
53	
54	
55	def text_from_content(value: Any) -> str:
56	    if value is None:
57	        return ""
58	    if isinstance(value, str):
59	        return value
60	    if isinstance(value, list):
61	        parts: list[str] = []
62	        for item in value:
63	            if isinstance(item, str):
64	                parts.append(item)
65	            elif isinstance(item, dict):
66	                if "text" in item:
67	                    parts.append(text_from_content(item.get("text")))
68	                elif "content" in item:
69	                    parts.append(text_from_content(item.get("content")))
70	                elif "input" in item:
71	                    parts.append(text_from_content(item.get("input")))
72	        return "\n".join(p for p in parts if p)
73	    if isinstance(value, dict):
74	        for key in ("text", "content", "message", "input", "output"):
75	            if key in value:
76	                return text_from_content(value[key])
77	    return ""
78	
79	
80	def session_id_from_name(path: Path) -> str:
81	    match = re.search(r"([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12})", path.name, re.I)
82	    return match.group(1) if match else path.stem
83	
84	
85	def archive_markdown_path(repo_root: Path, markdown: str) -> Path:
86	    """Resolve an archive markdown path from index.jsonl (may use Windows separators)."""
87	    normalized = markdown.replace("\\", "/")
88	    return repo_root / normalized
89	
90	
91	def slugify(value: str, max_len: int = 90) -> str:
92	    value = re.sub(r"[^\w.\- ]+", "-", value, flags=re.ASCII)
93	    value = re.sub(r"\s+", "-", value.strip())
94	    value = value.strip(".-_")
95	    return (value[:max_len].strip(".-_") or "session").lower()
